, and we deduce by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality that

\displaystyle\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{4\beta}|\overline{u}|^{2}dy

\displaystyle\leq-\frac{1}{(2\beta+1)^{2}-2}\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta}\overline{u}|y|^{2\beta+2}\Delta\overline{u}dy

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{(2\beta+1)^{2}-2}\left(\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{4\beta}|\overline{u}|^{2}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{4\beta+4}(\Delta\overline{u})^{2}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}.

(4.16)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{4\beta}|\overline{u}|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{((2\beta+1)^{2}-2)^{2}}\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{4\beta+4}(\Delta\overline{u})^{2}dy.

(4.17)

Notice that this inequality is valid for all

\displaystyle\beta>\frac{\sqrt{2}-1}{2}=0.207\cdots.

Making the change of variable
\alpha=4\beta
, we deduce that for all
u\in W^{2,2}_{0}(B(0,1))
, and for all
\alpha>2(\sqrt{2}-1)=0.8282\cdots
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{B(0,1)}|x|^{\alpha}u^{2}dx\leq\frac{16}{\left(\alpha^{2}+4\alpha-4\right)^{2}}\int_{B(0,1)}|x|^{\alpha+4}\left(\Delta u\right)^{2}dx.

(4.18)

Now, if
\overline{u}(x)=u(\iota(x))
, then in complex coordinates, we have

\displaystyle\overline{u}(z)=u\left(\frac{z}{|z|^{2}}\right)=u\left(\frac{\mathrm{Re}\,(z)}{|z|^{2}},\frac{\mathrm{Im}\,(z)}{|z|^{2}}\right)=u\left(\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\frac{1}{\overline{z}}\right),\mathrm{Im}\,\left(\frac{1}{\overline{z}}\right)\right)=u\left(\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1}{z}+\frac{1}{\overline{z}}\right),\frac{i}{2}\left(\frac{1}{z}-\frac{1}{\overline{z}}\right)\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{z}\overline{u}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2z^{2}}\partial_{x_{1}}u(\iota(z))-\frac{i}{2z^{2}}\partial_{x_{2}}u(\iota(z))

\displaystyle\partial_{z\overline{z}}^{2}\overline{u}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2z^{2}}\left(-\frac{1}{2\overline{z}^{2}}\partial_{x_{1}}^{2}u(\iota(z))+\frac{i}{2\overline{z}^{2}}\partial_{x_{1},x_{2}}^{2}u(\iota(z))\right)-\frac{i}{2z^{2}}\left(\frac{i}{2\overline{z}^{2}}\partial_{x_{2}}^{2}u(\iota(z))-\frac{1}{2z^{2}}\partial_{x_{1},x_{2}}^{2}u(\iota(z))\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4|z|^{4}}\Delta u(\iota(z))

\displaystyle\Delta\overline{u}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{|z|^{4}}\Delta u(\iota(z)).

(4.19)

Then, we get

\displaystyle\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{4\beta+4}\left(\Delta\overline{u}\right)^{2}dy

\displaystyle=a^{4}\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{4\beta}\left(\Delta u(a\,\iota(y))\right)^{2}\frac{dy}{|y|^{4}}=a^{2}\int_{\Omega}\left(\Delta u\right)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\beta}dx.

(4.20)

Thanks to (
4.17
) and (
4.20
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\beta}\frac{u^{2}}{|x|^{4}}dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{((2\beta+1)^{2}-2)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}\left(\Delta u\right)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\beta}dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{((2\beta+1)^{2}-2)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}\left(\Delta u\right)^{2}dx.

which concludes the proof of this step.

Step 3: Second integral, first part.

Now, consider for all
0<\beta<1
and for all
u\in W^{2,2}_{0}(\Omega)
the following integral

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\beta}\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}dx.

As above, making a change of variable
x=b\,y
yields for all
p>\frac{1}{\beta}

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\beta}\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{b^{2}}\int_{B_{1}\setminus\overline{B}_{b^{-1}a}(0)}|y|^{2\beta}\frac{|\nabla\overline{u}|^{2}}{|y|^{2}}dy=\frac{1}{b^{2}}\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta}\frac{|\nabla\overline{u}|^{2}}{|y|^{2}}dy

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{b^{2}}\left(\int_{B(0,1)}\frac{dy}{|y|^{2(1-\beta)p^{\prime}}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}}\left(\int_{B(0,1)}|\nabla\overline{u}|^{2p}\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{b^{2}}\frac{16}{\pi^{2-\frac{1}{p+1}}}C\left(2,\frac{2p}{p+1}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{1-(1-\beta)p^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}}\int_{B(0,1)}(\Delta\overline{u})^{2}dy

\displaystyle=\frac{16}{\pi^{2-\frac{1}{p+1}}}C\left(2,\frac{2p}{p+1}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{\pi}{1-(1-\beta)p^{\prime}}\right)^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta{u})^{2}dx.

(4.21)

Step 4: Second integral, inversion part.

Reasoning as above, we deduce that for all
\beta>0

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\beta}\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}dx=\frac{1}{a^{2}}\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2(\beta+1)}|\nabla\overline{u}|^{2}dy,

(4.22)

where
\overline{u}
is the extension by
0
of
u(b\,\iota(\,\cdot\,)):B_{1}\setminus\overline{B}_{b^{-1}a}(0)\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
. Integrating by parts as previously, we get

\displaystyle\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta+2}|\nabla\overline{u}|^{2}dy

\displaystyle=-\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta+2}\overline{u}\,\Delta\overline{u}\,dy-(2\beta+2)\int_{B(0,1)}{\overline{u}}\,({y}\cdot\nabla\overline{u})\,|y|^{2\beta}dy

\displaystyle=-\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta+2}\overline{u}\,\Delta\overline{u}\,dy+2\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta}|\overline{u}|^{2}dy.

(4.23)

Using (
4.18
) and assuming that
\alpha=2\beta>2(\sqrt{2}-1)
, or
\beta>\sqrt{2}-1
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta}|\overline{u}|^{2}dy

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{((\beta+1)^{2}-2)^{2}}\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta+4}\left(\Delta\overline{u}\right)^{2}dy

\displaystyle=\frac{a^{2}}{((\beta+1)^{2}-2)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}\left(\Delta u\right)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\beta}dx,

(4.24)

while Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and (
4.1
) show that

\displaystyle-\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta+2}\overline{u}\,\Delta\overline{u}\,dy

\displaystyle\leq\left(\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta}|\overline{u}|^{2}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta+4}\left(\Delta\overline{u}\right)^{2}dy\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{(\beta+1)^{2}-2}\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta+4}\left(\Delta\overline{u}\right)^{2}dx.

(4.25)

Therefore, we get by (
4.1
), (
4.1
), and (
4.1
) for all
\beta>\sqrt{2}-1

\displaystyle\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta+2}|\nabla\overline{u}|^{2}dy

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{(\beta+1)^{2}-2}+\frac{2}{((\beta+1)^{2}-2)^{2}}\right)\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta+4}(\Delta\overline{u})^{2}dy

\displaystyle=\frac{(\beta+1)^{2}}{((\beta+1)^{2}-2)^{2}}\int_{B(0,1)}|y|^{2\beta+4}(\Delta\overline{u})^{2}dy.

(4.26)

Thanks to (
4.22
), the identity (
4.1
), and (
4.1
), we deduce that for all
\beta>\sqrt{2}-1

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\beta}\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\beta+1)^{2}}{((\beta+1)^{2}-2)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta u)^{2}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\beta}dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(\beta+1)^{2}}{((\beta+1)^{2}-2)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta u)^{2}dx,

which concludes the proof of the theorem.
∎

We will also need a version of this theorem for functions that do not necessarily vanish on the boundary.
Thanks to the inequality from Theorem
4.2
, if
0<a<b<\infty
and
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)
, for all
\sqrt{2}-1<\beta<1
and for all
u\in W^{2,2}_{0}(\Omega)
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla u|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\beta}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\beta}\right)dx\leq C_{\beta}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta u)^{2}dx.

(4.27)

However, in one of the key lemmas (Lemma
IV.1
) used in the proof of the main theorem of
[
8
]
that we adapt to the case of Willmore immersions in
[
13
, Lemma
4.4
]
, we need such an estimate for functions that do not necessarily vanish on the boundary of neck regions. This new technical difficulty is due to terms in the second derivative that can only be bounded from below by

\displaystyle-\int_{\Sigma}|du|_{g}^{2}|A|^{2}d\mathrm{vol}_{g},

and those contributions can
a priori
prevent one from bounded from below the minimal eigenvalue of a sequence of immersions, an estimate that is crucial in the proof (see
[
8
, Lemma IV.
4
]
). Thanks to the inequality (
4.27
), the proof reduces to a delicate estimate involving biharmonic functions.

Theorem 4.3
.

There exists a universal constant
\Gamma<\infty
with the following property. Let
0<a<b<\infty
and
\Omega=B_{b}\setminus\overline{B}_{a}(0)
. For all
\dfrac{1}{2}<\beta<1

and for all
0<\gamma<1
, for all biharmonic function
\psi\in W^{2,2}(\Omega)

(
i.e.
that satisfies
\Delta^{2}\psi=0
on
\Omega
)
, assuming that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\max\left\{2,\frac{1}{2\beta-1}\log\left(4\beta\right),\frac{1}{4\beta}\log\left(\frac{2}{2-\sqrt{3}}\right),\frac{1}{4(1-\beta)}\log\left(1+\frac{8\beta(1-\beta)}{(2\beta-1)^{2}}\right),\frac{1}{4\beta}\log(8\beta(\beta+1))\right\},

(4.28)

we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\psi|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\Gamma}{\gamma(1-\gamma)}\frac{1}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\beta}+\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\beta}\right)dx

\displaystyle+\frac{\Gamma}{\gamma(1-\gamma)}\frac{1}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\beta}}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx.

(4.29)

Remark 4.4
.

The difficulty of this estimate lies in the fact that if one write
\psi=\psi_{0}+|x|^{2}\psi_{1}
, where
\psi_{0}
and
\psi_{1}
are harmonic functions, the frequencies of
\psi_{0}
and
\psi_{1}
have a non-trivial interaction. Furthermore, due to the introduction of the weight, some frequencies that disappear in the integration of
\nabla^{2}\psi
need to be controlled by the weighted
L^{2}
norm of
\psi
(that captures all frequencies thanks to the shift of the radial component). Last, but not least, controlling the two logarithmic components is delicate due to the involved algebraic expressions of them in the three integrals.

Proof.

Step 1:
Estimation of

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\psi|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}dx

As
\psi
is a biharmonic function, there exists harmonic functions
\psi_{0},\psi_{1}:\Omega\rightarrow\mathbb{R}
such that
\psi=\psi_{0}+|x|^{2}\psi_{1}
. Therefore, there exists
\alpha,\beta\in\mathbb{R}
and
\left\{a_{n}\right\}_{n\in\mathbb{Z}},\left\{b_{n}\right\}_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\subset\mathbb{C}
such that

\displaystyle\psi(z)=\alpha\log|z|+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)+|z|^{2}\left(\beta\log|z|+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right).

(4.30)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{z}\psi

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2z}\left(\alpha+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n\,a_{n}z^{n}+2|z|^{2}\left(\beta\log|z|+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)+|z|^{2}\left(\beta+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n\,b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2z}\left(\alpha+2\beta|z|^{2}\log|z|+(\beta+2b_{0})|z|^{2}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n\,a_{n}z^{n}+|z|^{2}\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}(n+1)b_{n}z^{n}+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\overline{b_{n}}\overline{z}^{n}\right)\right).

The radial component of the gradient is therefore given by

\displaystyle\mathrm{rad}\left(|\nabla\psi|^{2}\right)=\mathrm{rad}\left(4|\partial_{z}\psi|^{2}\right)=\frac{1}{|z|^{2}}\bigg\{\alpha^{2}+4\alpha\beta|z|^{2}\log|z|+2\alpha(\beta+2b_{0})|z|^{2}+4\beta^{2}|z|^{4}\log^{2}|z|

\displaystyle+4\beta(\beta+2b_{0})|z|^{4}\log|z|+(\beta+2b_{0})^{2}|z|^{4}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|n|^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2n}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}((n+1)^{2}+4)|b_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2(n+2)}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n(n+1)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)|z|^{2(n+1)}\bigg\}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\psi|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}dx=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}\frac{1}{r^{3}}\bigg\{\alpha^{2}+4\alpha\beta r^{2}\log(r)+2\alpha(\beta+2b_{0})r^{2}+4\beta^{2}r^{4}\log^{2}(r)

\displaystyle+4\beta(\beta+2b_{0})r^{4}\log(r)+(\beta+2b_{0})^{2}r^{4}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|n|^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{2n}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\left((n+1)^{2}+4\right)|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2(n+2)}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n(n+1)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)r^{2(n+1)}\bigg\}\left(\frac{r}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}dx

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi\alpha^{2}}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\right)+\frac{4\alpha\beta}{\gamma}\left(\log(b)-\log(a)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)+\frac{2\pi\alpha(\beta+2b_{0})}{\gamma}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\pi\beta^{2}}{\gamma+1}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\frac{\log(b)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{\log(a)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\pi\beta(\beta+2b_{0})}{\gamma+1}\left(b^{2}\left(\log(b)-\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log(a)-\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi(\beta+2b_{0})^{2}}{\gamma+1}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{|n|^{2}}{n-1+\gamma}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{((n+1)^{2}+4)}{n+1+\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{n(n+1)}{n+\gamma}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\left(b^{2n}-a^{2n}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right).

Recall that

\displaystyle\psi(z)=\alpha\log|z|+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}a_{n}z^{n}\right)+|z|^{2}\left(\beta\log|z|+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}z^{n}\right)\right).

We compute

\displaystyle\mathrm{rad}(\psi^{2})

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}\log^{2}|z|+2\alpha\,a_{0}\log|z|+2\alpha\beta|z|^{2}\log^{2}|z|+2(\alpha\,b_{0}+\beta\,a_{0})|z|^{2}\log|z|+\beta^{2}|z|^{4}\log^{2}|z|

\displaystyle+2\beta\,b_{0}|z|^{4}\log|z|+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}|a_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2n}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}|b_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2(n+2)}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)|z|^{2(n+1)}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}dx=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}\frac{1}{r^{3}}\bigg\{\alpha^{2}\log^{2}(r)+2\alpha\,a_{0}\log(r)+2\alpha\beta r^{2}\log^{2}(r)+2(\alpha\,b_{0}+\beta\,a_{0})r^{2}\log(r)

\displaystyle+\beta^{2}r^{4}\log^{2}(r)+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{2n}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2(n+2)}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)r^{2(n+1)}\bigg\}\left(\frac{r}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}dr

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi\alpha^{2}}{2\gamma-1}\left(\frac{1}{b^{2}}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\frac{\log(b)}{2\gamma-1}+\frac{1}{2(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\right)-\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{\log(a)}{2\gamma-1}+\frac{1}{2(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi\alpha\,a_{0}}{2\gamma-1}\left(\frac{1}{b^{2}}\left(\log(b)-\frac{1}{2(2\gamma-1)}\right)-\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\log(a)-\frac{1}{2(2\gamma-1)}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi\alpha\beta}{\gamma}\left(\left(\log^{2}(b)-\frac{\log(b)}{2\gamma}+\frac{1}{4\gamma^{2}}\right)-\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{\log(a)}{2\gamma^{2}}+\frac{1}{4\gamma}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi(\alpha\,b_{0}+\beta\,a_{0})}{\gamma}\left(\log(b)-\frac{1}{4\gamma}-\left(\log(a)-\frac{1}{4\gamma}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi\beta^{2}}{2\gamma+1}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\frac{\log(b)}{2\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{\log(a)}{2\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\pi\beta\,b_{0}}{2\gamma+1}\left(b^{2}\left(\log(b)-\frac{1}{2\gamma+1}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log(a)-\frac{1}{2\gamma+1}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{1}{n-1+2\gamma}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{1}{n+1+2\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{1}{n+2\gamma}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\left(b^{2n}-a^{2n}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

Now, we have

\displaystyle\Delta\psi

\displaystyle=\left(\partial_{r}^{2}+\frac{1}{r}+\frac{1}{r^{2}}\partial_{\theta}^{2}\right)\left(\beta\,r^{2}\log(r)+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}b_{n}r^{n+2}e^{in\theta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=4\beta(\log(r)+1)+\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\left((n+2)(n+1)+(n+2)-n^{2}\right)b_{n}r^{n}e^{in\theta}\right)

\displaystyle=4\beta(\log(r)+1)+4\,\mathrm{Re}\,\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}(n+1)b_{n}r^{n}e^{in\theta}\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}\left(16r(\beta\log(r)+\beta+b_{0})^{2}+8\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}(n+1)^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2n+1}\right)dr

\displaystyle=16\pi\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\log(b)+\frac{1}{2}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\log(a)+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+32\pi\beta(\beta+b_{0})\left(b^{2}\left(\log(b)-\frac{1}{2}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log(a)-\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)+16\pi(\beta+b_{0})^{2}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+8\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{-1,0\right\}}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\right|.

(4.31)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\partial_{z}^{2}\psi=\partial_{z}\left(\frac{1}{2z}\left(\alpha+2\beta|z|^{2}\log|z|+(\beta+2b_{0})|z|^{2}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n\,a_{n}z^{n}+|z|^{2}\left(\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}(n+1)b_{n}z^{n}+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\overline{b_{n}}\overline{z}^{n}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2z^{2}}\left(-\alpha+\beta|z|^{2}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n(n-1)a_{n}z^{n}+|z|^{2}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n(n+1)b_{n}z^{n}\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{rad}\left(|\partial_{z}^{2}\psi|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4|z|^{4}}\left(\alpha^{2}-2\alpha\beta|z|^{2}+\beta^{2}|z|^{4}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}(n-1)^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2n}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}(n+1)^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2(n+2)}\right.

\displaystyle+\left.+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}(n^{2}-1)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)|z|^{2(n+1)}\right),

and

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}4|\partial_{z}^{2}\psi|^{2}dx=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}\frac{1}{r^{3}}\left(\alpha^{2}-2\alpha\beta r^{2}+\beta^{2}r^{4}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}(n-1)^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{2n}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}(n+1)^{2}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2(n+2)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n^{2}(n^{2}-1)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)r^{2(n+1)}\right)dr=\pi\alpha^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\right)-4\pi\alpha\beta\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\pi\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,1\right\}}n^{2}|n-1||a_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\right|+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{-1,0\right\}}n^{2}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\right|

\displaystyle+2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{-1,0,1\right\}}n(n^{2}-1)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\left(b^{2n}-a^{2n}\right).

We first estimate since
\gamma\notin\mathbb{N}

\displaystyle\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{((n+1)^{2}+4)}{n+1+\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{4}{\gamma}|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4}{\gamma}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,-1\right\}}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right|.

Then, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\right|=\sum_{n\geq 0}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\geq 2}|n-1||b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)\left(\sum_{n\geq 0}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+1)}+\sum_{n\geq 2}|n-1||b_{-n}|\frac{1}{a^{2(n-1)}}\right).

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right|=\sum_{n\geq 0}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1+\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\geq 2}|n-1||b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-1)}}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\sum_{n\geq 0}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\geq 2}|n-1||b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\right|

where we used the following inequality (valid for all
n\geq 0
)

\displaystyle\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1+\gamma)}\right)\leq\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1)}\right).

Indeed, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1+\gamma)}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1)}}=\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}+\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1)}},

which is minimal for
n=0
. Finally, we deduce that

\displaystyle\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{((n+1)^{2}+4)}{n+1+\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{4\pi}{\gamma}|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\pi}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\pi}{\gamma}|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)+\frac{1}{2\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx.

(4.32)

Other estimates are similar. First, we have

\displaystyle 2\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx=\int_{\Omega}4|\partial_{z}^{2}\psi|^{2}dx+\frac{1}{4}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx=\pi\alpha^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\right)-4\pi\alpha\beta\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\pi\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,1\right\}}n^{2}|n-1||a_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\right|+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{-1,0\right\}}n^{2}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\right|

\displaystyle+2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{-1,0,1\right\}}n(n^{2}-1)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\left(b^{2n}-a^{2n}\right)

\displaystyle+4\pi\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\log(b)+\frac{1}{2}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\log(a)+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+8\pi\beta(\beta+b_{0})\left(b^{2}\left(\log(b)-\frac{1}{2}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log(a)-\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)+4\pi(\beta+b_{0})^{2}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{-1,0\right\}}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\right|

\displaystyle=\pi\alpha^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\right)-4\pi\alpha\beta\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\pi\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+4\pi\left(b^{2}\left(\beta^{2}\log^{2}(b)+\beta(\beta+b_{0})\log(b)+(\beta+b_{0})\left(\frac{1}{2}\beta+b_{0}\right)\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-a^{2}\left(\beta^{2}\log^{2}\left(a\right)+\beta(\beta+b_{0})\log\left(a\right)+\left(\beta+b_{0}\right)\left(\frac{1}{2}\beta+b_{0}\right)\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,1\right\}}n^{2}|n-1||a_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\right|+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{-1,0\right\}}(n^{2}+1)|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\right|

\displaystyle+2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{-1,0,1\right\}}n(n^{2}-1)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\left(b^{2n}-a^{2n}\right).

Now, we have to be particularly careful to show that the
L^{2}
norm of
\nabla^{2}\psi
bounds the sum in
\left\{a_{n}\right\}_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}
. Notice that for all
n\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle n^{2}|n-1||a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-1)}+2n(n^{2}-1)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)b^{2n}+(n^{2}+1)|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+1)}

\displaystyle\geq\left(n^{2}(n-1)-\frac{n^{2}(n+1)^{2}(n-1)^{2}}{\left(\sqrt{(n^{2}+1)(n+1)}\right)^{2}}\right)|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-1)}

\displaystyle=n^{2}(n-1)\left(1-\frac{(n+1)(n-1)}{n^{2}+1}\right)|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-1)}=\frac{2n^{2}(n-1)}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-1)}.

Let
n\geq 2
. Then, we have

\displaystyle 2n(n^{2}-1)|a_{n}||b_{n}||b^{2n}-a^{2n}|=2n(n^{2}-1)|a_{n}||b_{n}|b^{2n}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{n^{2}(n+1)(n-1)^{2}}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2n}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}\right)+(n^{2}+1)(n+1)|b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+1)}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}}.

Therefore, we get for all
n\geq 2
(for
n=1
the middle coefficient vanishes and the inequality is trivial)

\displaystyle n^{2}|n-1||a_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\right|+2n(n^{2}-1)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\left(b^{2n}-a^{2n}\right)+(n^{2}+1)|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+1)}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2n^{2}|n-1|}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}\right)+\frac{(n^{2}+1)|n+1|}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1)}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2n}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{2n^{2}|n-1|}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}\right)-(n^{2}+1)|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\frac{\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{n-1}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{n+1}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}}

\displaystyle=\frac{2n^{2}|n-1|}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}\right)-(n^{2}+1)|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{2n^{2}|n-1|}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}\right)-(n^{2}+1)|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right).

Now, we compute

\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}(n^{2}+1)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}=\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\frac{5-5\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}}.

Since

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx\geq 8\pi\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)\sum_{n\geq 2}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+1)},

we deduce that

\displaystyle 2\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx+\frac{1}{8}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\frac{5-5\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx

\displaystyle\geq 2\pi\sum_{n\geq 2}\frac{n^{2}|n-1|}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n-1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n-1)}\right).

Reasoning similarly for negative frequencies, we deduce in fact that

\displaystyle 2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,1\right\}}\frac{n^{2}|n-1|}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}|b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}|\leq 2\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{8}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\frac{5-5\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx,

(4.33)

that we rewrite more simply as

\displaystyle 2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,1\right\}}\frac{n^{2}|n-1|}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\right|\leq 2\left(1+\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\frac{5-5\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}}\right)\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{|n|^{2}}{|n-1+\gamma|}|a_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right|\leq\frac{\pi}{\gamma}|a_{1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{5\pi}{\gamma}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,1\right\}}\frac{n^{2}|n-1|}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\pi}{\gamma}|a_{1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)+\frac{5\pi}{1-\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,1\right\}}\frac{n^{2}|n-1|}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\pi}{\gamma}|a_{1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)+\frac{5}{1-\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\left(1+\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\frac{5-5\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}}\right)\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx.

Now, we move to the estimation of the bound the logarithm contributions. We trivially have

\displaystyle\frac{\pi\alpha^{2}}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\right)\leq\frac{\pi\alpha^{2}}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\right).

Then,

\displaystyle\frac{4\pi\beta^{2}}{\gamma+1}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\frac{\log(b)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{\log(a)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

needs to be compared to

\displaystyle 16\pi\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\log(b)+\frac{1}{2}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\log(a)+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right).

Since we have assumed that
0<a<b<1
, we trivially estimate

\displaystyle b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\frac{\log(b)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)=b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)+\frac{|\log(b)|}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)\leq b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\log(b)+\frac{1}{2}\right).

On the other hand,

\displaystyle a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{\log(a)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)\geq\frac{1}{(\gamma+1)^{2}}a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\log(a)+\frac{1}{2}\right).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{4\pi\beta^{2}}{\gamma+1}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\frac{\log(b)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{\log(a)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\pi\beta^{2}}{(\gamma+1)^{3}}\left((\gamma+1)^{2}b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\log(b)+\frac{1}{2}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\log(a)+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right).

Recalling that

\displaystyle\mathrm{rad}\left(z\,\partial_{z}\psi\right)=\frac{1}{2}\left(\alpha+2\beta|z|^{2}\log|z|+(\beta+b_{0})|z|^{2}\right),

we deduce by the triangle inequality and the elementary inequality
(a+b+c)^{2}\leq 4(a^{2}+b^{2}+c^{2})
that

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\psi|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}dx\leq\frac{4\pi\alpha^{2}}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\right)+\frac{16\pi\beta^{2}}{\gamma+1}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}\left(b\right)-\frac{\log(b)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{\log(a)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)+\frac{4\pi(\beta+2b_{0})^{2}}{\gamma+1}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)^{2\gamma}

\displaystyle+2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{|n|^{2}}{n-1+\gamma}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{((n+1)^{2}+4)}{n+1+\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right).

(4.34)

Then, we need to bound
a_{1}
and
b_{-1}
by the weighted
L^{2}
norm of
\psi
. Notice that we have

\displaystyle\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{1}{n-1+2\gamma}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{1}{n+1+2\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{1}{n+2\gamma}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\left(b^{2n}-a^{2n}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)\leq\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}dx,

(4.35)

and since each term is positive (since it corresponds to the integration of a squared quantity), we deduce that for all
n\in\mathbb{Z}
, we also have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n-1+2\gamma}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)+\frac{1}{n+1+2\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{n+2\gamma}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\left(b^{2n}-a^{2n}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}dx.

(4.36)

For
n=-1
, the inequality becomes

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(1-\gamma)}|a_{-1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4}}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}\right)+\frac{1}{2\gamma}|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{2\gamma-1}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}\overline{b_{-1}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)\leq\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{x}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}dx.

(4.37)

Thanks to Cauchy’s inequality, we have

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{2\gamma-1}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}\overline{b_{-1}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)\geq-\frac{1}{2(1-\gamma)}|a_{-1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4}}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2(1-\gamma)}{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}|b_{-1}|^{2}\frac{\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}.

Now, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)-\frac{2(1-\gamma)}{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\frac{\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}=\frac{1}{2\gamma(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\frac{1}{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\times\left((2\gamma-1)^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}\right)-4\gamma(1-\gamma)\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\right).

Notice that the quantity in parentheses is strictly negative for
\gamma=1/2
and vanishes for
\gamma=1
. More precisely, as
\gamma\rightarrow 1
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1-\gamma}{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}=\frac{1}{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}}\frac{1-\gamma}{1-e^{-4(1-\gamma)\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}}\underset{\gamma\rightarrow 1}{\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)^{4}\frac{1}{4\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}.

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)-\frac{2(1-\gamma)}{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\frac{\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}

\displaystyle\underset{\gamma\rightarrow 1}{\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow}\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}\right)-\frac{1}{2\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)^{4}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}\right)-\frac{1}{2\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}\underset{\frac{b}{a}\rightarrow\infty}{\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow}\frac{1}{2}>0.

Therefore, for
\gamma<1
and
\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)>1
large enough, this constant will be positive, which will give us a control of
b_{-1}
! Let us now get explicit estimates for which our function is positive.

We also notice if
x=\dfrac{a}{b}
that

\displaystyle\frac{(x^{4\gamma}-x^{2})^{2}}{x^{4\gamma}-x^{4}}=x^{4(1-\gamma)}\frac{(1-x^{2(2\gamma-1)})^{2}}{1-x^{4(1-\gamma)}}\underset{x\rightarrow 0}{\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow}0.

Therefore, for all fixed
\dfrac{1}{2}<\gamma<1
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)-\frac{2(1-\gamma)}{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\frac{\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}\underset{\frac{b}{a}\rightarrow\infty}{\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow}\frac{1}{2\gamma}>0.

Therefore, for all
\dfrac{1}{2}<\gamma<1
, there exists
R_{\gamma}<\infty
such that for all
0<a<b<\infty
such that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq R_{\gamma}^{\prime},

we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)-\frac{2(1-\gamma)}{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\frac{\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}\geq\frac{1}{4\gamma}.

Let us estimate some
R_{\gamma}
(that gives a similar inequality). Since

\displaystyle\frac{(x^{4\gamma}-x^{2})^{2}}{x^{4\gamma}-x^{4}}=x^{4(1-\gamma)}\frac{(1-x^{2(2\gamma-1)})^{2}}{1-x^{4(1-\gamma)}}\leq\frac{x^{4(1-\gamma)}}{1-x^{4(1-\gamma)}},

we notice that

\displaystyle\frac{x^{4(1-\gamma)}}{1-x^{4(1-\gamma)}}\leq\frac{1}{4\gamma}\frac{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}{2\gamma(1-\gamma)}

if and only if

\displaystyle x^{4(1-\gamma)}\leq\frac{\frac{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}{8\gamma(1-\gamma)}}{\frac{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}{8\gamma(1-\gamma)}+1}=\frac{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}{(2\gamma-1)^{2}+8\gamma(1-\gamma)}

which gives the condition

\displaystyle\frac{b}{a}\geq\left(\frac{(2\gamma-1)^{2}+8\gamma(1-\gamma)}{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{4(1-\gamma)}}

and finally

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4(1-\gamma)}\log\left(1+\frac{8\gamma(1-\gamma)}{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\right).

(4.38)

Therefore, we get the estimate

\displaystyle R_{\gamma}\leq\frac{1}{4(1-\gamma)}\log\left(1+\frac{8\gamma(1-\gamma)}{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\right)

which blows us as
\gamma\rightarrow\dfrac{1}{2}
and
\gamma\rightarrow 1
. Summarising, assuming that (
4.38
) holds, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)-\frac{2(1-\gamma)}{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\frac{\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{2}}{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}\geq\frac{1}{4\gamma}\left(1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)>0

provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4\gamma}\log(2)

that is maximal at
\gamma=\dfrac{1}{2}
. An easy estimate shows that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4(1-\gamma)}\log\left(1+\frac{8\gamma(1-\gamma)}{(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2}\log(2)\quad\text{for all}\;\,\frac{1}{2}<\gamma<1,

so the second condition is always satisfied provided that (
4.38
) holds. Therefore, we finally deduce that (
4.38
) implies that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4\gamma}|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{1}{2(1-\gamma)}|a_{-1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4}}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}\right)+\frac{1}{2\gamma}|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{2}{2\gamma-1}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}\overline{b_{-1}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)\leq\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}dx,

which shows that

\displaystyle\frac{4\pi}{\gamma}|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)\leq 16\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}dx.

More generally, for all
\dfrac{1}{2}<\delta<1
, if

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4(1-\delta)}\log\left(1+\frac{8\delta(1-\delta)}{(2\delta-1)^{2}}\right),

(4.39)

we get

\displaystyle\frac{4\pi}{\gamma}|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{16\,\delta}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\delta}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx.

(4.40)

Now, we move to the estimate of
|a_{1}|^{2}
. Taking (
4.1
) for
n=1
yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma}|a_{1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)}|b_{1}|^{2}b^{4}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(\gamma+1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{2\gamma+1}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{b_{1}}\right)b^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)\leq\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}dx.

As previously, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma}|a_{1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)}|b_{1}|^{2}b^{4}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(\gamma+1)}\right)

(4.41)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{2\gamma+1}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{b_{1}}\right)b^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq|a_{1}|^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2\gamma}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)-\frac{2(\gamma+1)}{(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(\gamma+1)}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{2\gamma(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\left((2\gamma+1)^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(\gamma+1)}\right)-4\gamma(\gamma+1)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{2\gamma(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\left(\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(\gamma+1)}\right)-4\gamma(\gamma+1)\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\gamma+1)}\right)^{2}\right).

(4.42)

Notice that the quantity within parentheses converges to
1
as
\dfrac{b}{a}\rightarrow\infty
.
We trivially estimate

\displaystyle\left(x^{2\gamma}-x^{2(\gamma+1)}\right)^{2}=x^{4\gamma}\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{2}\leq x^{4\gamma}\leq\frac{1}{8\gamma(\gamma+1)}

if and only if

\displaystyle\frac{b}{a}\geq\left(8\gamma(\gamma+1)\right)^{\frac{1}{4\gamma}},

or

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4\gamma}\log\left(8\gamma(\gamma+1)\right).

(4.43)

Provided that this estimate holds, we get

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{2\gamma(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\left(\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(\gamma+1)}\right)-4\gamma(\gamma+1)\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(\gamma+1)}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{2\gamma(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\left(\frac{1}{2}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(\gamma+1)}+\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(2\gamma+1)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{2\gamma(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\left(1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)>0

provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4\gamma}\log(2),

(4.44)

which is implied by (
4.43
). Therefore, for all
0<a<b<\infty
satisfying (
4.43
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{|a_{1}|^{2}}{2\gamma(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\left(1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}dx,

and finally

\displaystyle\frac{\pi}{\gamma}|a_{1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)\leq 2(2\gamma+1)^{2}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}dx.

More generally, for all
\dfrac{1}{2}<\delta<1
, provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{4\delta}\log\left(8\delta(\delta+1)\right),

(4.45)

we have

\displaystyle\frac{\pi}{\gamma}|a_{1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{2\delta(2\delta+1)^{2}}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\delta}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx.

(4.46)

Now, coming back to the
L^{2}
norm of
\partial_{z}^{2}\psi
, recall that

\displaystyle\pi\alpha^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\right)-4\pi\alpha\beta\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\pi\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\right)\leq 4\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{z}^{2}\psi|^{2}dx.

We trivially estimate

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\right)-4\alpha\beta\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\right)\geq\alpha^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}-\frac{4\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)}{b^{2}-a^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}-\frac{4\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right).

Since

\displaystyle\lim_{x\rightarrow 0}\left(1-x^{2}-\frac{4x^{2}}{1-x^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{1}{x}\right)\right)=1

for
\dfrac{b}{a}
large enough, we get

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{2}}{2}\frac{1}{a^{2}}\leq\frac{4}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{z}^{2}\psi|^{2}dx.

Let us find an explicit estimate of the conformal class for which this inequality holds. We have

\displaystyle 1-x^{2}-\frac{4x^{2}}{1-x^{2}}\log^{2}(x)\geq\frac{1}{2}

if and only if

\displaystyle 2(1-x^{2})^{2}-8x^{2}\log^{2}(x)\geq 1-x^{2},

or

\displaystyle(1-x^{2})(1-2x^{2})-8x^{2}\log^{2}(x)\geq 0.

Making a change of variable
\log(x)=-t
, we are led to study the function

\displaystyle f(t)=\left(1-e^{-2t}\right)\left(1-2e^{-2t}\right)-8t^{2}e^{-2t}=1+2e^{-4t}-\left(3+8t^{2}\right)e^{-2t}.

We have

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(t)=-8e^{-4t}-16te^{-2t}+(6+16t^{2})e^{-2t}=-8e^{-4t}+(6-16t+16t^{2})e^{-2t}.

We have
f^{\prime}(t)>0
provided that
6-16t+16t^{2}\geq 8
, or
8t^{2}-8t-1\geq 0
, which holds true for

\displaystyle t\geq\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{2}\sqrt{\frac{3}{2}}=1.11237\cdots

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle f(2)=1+2e^{-8}-35e^{-4}=e^{-8}\left(e^{8}+2-35e^{4}\right)>e^{-4}\left(e^{4}-35\right)>0,

where the last inequality holds (for example) by the elementary estimate
e>2.7
. Therefore, we have
f(t)>0
for all
t\geq 2
. In other words, for all
x\leq e^{-2}
, we have

\displaystyle 1-x^{2}-\frac{4x^{2}}{1-x^{2}}\log^{2}(x)\geq\frac{1}{2}.

Summarising, provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq 2,

(4.47)

we have

\displaystyle\frac{\alpha^{2}}{a^{2}}\leq\frac{8}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{z}^{2}\psi|^{2}dx.

(4.48)

Finally, provided that (
4.47
) holds, we get

\displaystyle\frac{4\pi\alpha^{2}}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\right)\leq\frac{32}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{z}^{2}\psi|^{2}dx\leq\frac{16}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx.

(4.49)

Then, we have

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\right)-4\alpha\beta\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)-4\alpha\beta\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)+\beta^{2}(b^{2}-a^{2})\geq\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}-\frac{8\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}(b^{2}-a^{2}).

If the conformal class satisfies the inequality (
4.47
), we deduce in particular that

\displaystyle\frac{4x^{2}}{1-x^{2}}\log^{2}(x)\leq\frac{1}{2}-x^{2},

which shows that

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}-\frac{8\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\log^{2}\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\right)+\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}(b^{2}-a^{2})\geq\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}(b^{2}-a^{2})+\alpha^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\geq\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}(b^{2}-a^{2}).

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\beta^{2}(b^{2}-a^{2})\leq\frac{8}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{z}^{2}\psi|^{2}.

(4.50)

Now, in order to estimate the logarithm contributions, we need to bound
b_{0}
. Applying (
4.1
) to
n=0
, we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma-1}a_{0}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b^{2}}-\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)+\frac{1}{2\gamma+1}b_{0}^{2}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\gamma}a_{0}b_{0}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}dx.

We have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma-1}a_{0}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{b^{2}}-\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)+\frac{1}{2\gamma+1}b_{0}^{2}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)+\frac{1}{\gamma}a_{0}b_{0}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\gamma-1}a_{0}^{2}\frac{1}{b^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma-1)}\right)+\frac{1}{2\gamma+1}b_{0}^{2}b^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)+\frac{1}{\gamma}a_{0}b_{0}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\geq b_{0}^{2}b^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2\gamma+1}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)-\frac{2\gamma-1}{4\gamma^{2}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma-1)}}\right).

Notice that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma+1}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)-\frac{2\gamma-1}{4\gamma^{2}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma-1)}}\underset{\frac{b}{a}\rightarrow\infty}{\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow}\frac{1}{2\gamma+1}-\frac{2\gamma-1}{4\gamma^{2}}=\frac{1}{4\gamma^{2}(2\gamma+1)}>0.

Let us now find an estimate on the conformal class under which this function is strictly positive. We have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma+1}\left(1-x^{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)-\frac{2\gamma-1}{4\gamma^{2}}\frac{\left(1-x^{4\gamma}\right)^{2}}{1-x^{2(2\gamma-1)}}\geq\frac{1}{8\gamma^{2}(2\gamma+1)}

(4.51)

if and only if

\displaystyle 8\gamma^{2}(1-x^{2(2\gamma+1)}-x^{2(2\gamma-1)}+x^{8\gamma})-2(4\gamma^{2}-1)\left(1-2x^{4\gamma}+x^{8\gamma}\right)\geq 1-x^{2(2\gamma-1)}.

Since

\displaystyle 8\gamma^{2}(1-x^{2(2\gamma+1)}-x^{2(2\gamma-1)}+x^{8\gamma})-2(4\gamma^{2}-1)\left(1-2x^{4\gamma}+x^{8\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle=-8\gamma^{2}\left(x^{2\gamma-1}-x^{2\gamma+1}\right)^{2}+2\left(1-x^{4\gamma}\right)^{2}=2\left(\left(1-x^{4\gamma}\right)^{2}-4\gamma^{2}x^{2(2\gamma-1)}\left(1-x^{2}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 2\left(\left(1-x^{4\gamma}\right)^{2}-4\gamma^{2}x^{2(2\gamma-1)}\right)

we deduce that the inequality

\displaystyle\left(1-x^{4\gamma}\right)^{2}-4\gamma^{2}x^{2(2\gamma-1)}\geq\frac{1}{2}

implies (
4.51
). For all

\displaystyle x\leq\left(\frac{1}{16\gamma^{2}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2(2\gamma-1)}}=\left(\frac{1}{4\gamma}\right)^{\frac{1}{2\gamma-1}},

we have

\displaystyle\left(1-x^{4\gamma}\right)^{2}-4\gamma^{2}x^{2(2\gamma-1)}\geq\left(1-x^{4\gamma}\right)^{2}-\frac{1}{4}\geq\frac{1}{2}\Longleftrightarrow x\leq\left(1-\frac{\sqrt{3}}{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{4\gamma}}.

Therefore, provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\max\left\{\frac{1}{2\gamma-1}\log\left(4\gamma\right),\frac{1}{4\gamma}\log\left(\frac{2}{2-\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\},

(4.52)

the inequality (
4.51
) holds and we deduce in particular that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{8\gamma^{2}(2\gamma+1)}b_{0}^{2}\,b^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}dx.

(4.53)

Finally
,

\displaystyle 16\pi\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\log(b)+\frac{1}{2}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\log(a)+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+32\pi\beta(\beta+b_{0})\left(b^{2}\left(\log(b)-\frac{1}{2}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log(a)-\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)+16\pi(\beta+b_{0})^{2}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=16\pi\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)+\log(b)-\frac{1}{2}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)+\log(a)-\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+32\pi\beta b_{0}\left(b^{2}\log(b)-a^{2}\log(a)\right)+16\pi((\beta+b_{0})^{2}-\beta b_{0})(b^{2}-a^{2})\leq\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx.

Notice that we control
(\beta^{2}+b_{0})^{2}b^{2}
, which allows us to estimate

\displaystyle 16\pi\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)+\log(b)-\frac{1}{2}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)+\log(a)-\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+32\pi\beta b_{0}\left(b^{2}\log(b)-a^{2}\log(a)\right)+16\pi((\beta+b_{0})^{2}-\beta b_{0})(b^{2}-a^{2})

\displaystyle\geq 16\pi\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)+\log(b)\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)+\log(a)\right)\right)-8\pi\beta^{2}\left(\frac{(b^{2}\log(b)-a^{2}\log(a))^{2}}{b^{2}-a^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+16\pi\left((\beta+b_{0})^{2}-\beta\,b_{0}-2b_{0}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}\right)(b^{2}-a^{2})

Now, we have

\displaystyle 2\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)+\log(b)\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)+\log(a)\right)\right)-\frac{(b^{2}\log(b)-a^{2}\log(a))^{2}}{b^{2}-a^{2}}

\displaystyle=b^{2}\left(\left(2-\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)\log^{2}(b)+2\log(b)-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\left(2+\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)\log^{2}(a)-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\log(a)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\log(a)\log(b)\frac{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right),

which is positive is the conformal class is large enough. Indeed, if
b
is fixed, we have

\displaystyle\left(2-\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)\log^{2}(b)+2\log(b)-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\left(2+\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)\log^{2}(a)-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\log(a)

\displaystyle+2\log(a)\log(b)\frac{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\underset{a\rightarrow 0}{\mathrel{\mathchoice{{}\hbox{$\displaystyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\textstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\meno}$}}{{}\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\meno}$}}}\mathrel{\mkern-3.0mu}\rightarrow}\log^{2}(b)+2\log(b)>0

provided that
0<b<e^{-2}
(or
b>1
). Furthermore, for all
0<b<e^{-4}
, we have
\log^{2}(b)+2\log(b)>\dfrac{1}{2}\log^{2}(b)
, which implies that as
0<b<e^{-4}
is fixed and
0<a<b
is small enough, we have

\displaystyle 16\pi\beta^{2}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\log(b)+\frac{1}{2}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\log(a)+\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)

\displaystyle+32\pi\beta(\beta+b_{0})\left(b^{2}\left(\log(b)-\frac{1}{2}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log(a)-\frac{1}{2}\right)\right)+16\pi(\beta+b_{0})^{2}\left(b^{2}-a^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\geq 8\pi\beta^{2}\log^{2}(b)+16\pi\left((\beta+b_{0})^{2}-\beta\,b_{0}-2b_{0}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}\right)(b^{2}-a^{2})

\displaystyle=4\pi\beta^{2}\log^{2}(b)+16\pi\left((\beta+b_{0})^{2}-\beta\,b_{0}-2b_{0}^{2}-\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}\right)(b^{2}-a^{2})

\displaystyle=4\pi\beta^{2}b^{2}\log^{2}(b)+16\pi\left(\frac{1}{2}\beta^{2}+\beta\,b_{0}-b_{0}^{2}\right)(b^{2}-a^{2})\geq 8\pi\beta^{2}\log^{2}(b)-8\pi\,b_{0}^{2}(b^{2}-a^{2}),

which implies by (
4.53
) that for all
\dfrac{1}{2}<\delta<1
such that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\max\left\{\frac{1}{2\delta-1}\log\left(4\delta\right),\frac{1}{4\delta}\log\left(\frac{2}{2-\sqrt{3}}\right)\right\},

(4.54)

we have (for
0<b<e^{-4}
fixed and
a
small enough)

\displaystyle 4\pi b^{2}\beta^{2}\log^{2}(b)\leq 8\pi\,b_{0}^{2}(b^{2}-a^{2})+\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx\leq 64\delta^{2}(2\delta+1)\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx+\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx.

(4.55)

Now, let us estimate
0<a<b<e^{-2}
such that (
4.55
) holds. First, assuming that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2}\log(5),

(4.56)

we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\leq\frac{5}{4}

and since
0<a<b<1
, we have
\log(a)\log(b)>0
, so that

\displaystyle\left(2-\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)\log^{2}(b)+2\log(b)-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\left(2+\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)\log^{2}(a)-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\log(a)

\displaystyle+2\log(a)\log(b)\frac{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{3}{4}\log^{2}(b)+2\log(b)-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{13}{4}\log^{2}(a)-2\left(1+|\log(b)|\right)|\log(a)|\right)\geq\frac{1}{2}\log^{2}(b)+2\log(b)

if and only if

\displaystyle\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{13}{4}\log^{2}(a)-2\left(1+|\log(b)|\right)\log|a|\right)\leq\frac{1}{4}\log^{2}(b),

which is verified if

\displaystyle 13\,a^{2}\log^{2}(a)\leq b^{2}\log^{2}(b),

which is verified if and only if

\displaystyle 0<a\leq e^{W_{-1}\left(\frac{b\log(b)}{\sqrt{13}}\right)},

where
W_{-1}
is the negative branch of the Lambert function (the inverse of the function
x\mapstochar\rightarrow xe^{x}
on
[e^{-1},0[
). Recalling that the Lambert function admits the following asymptotic expansion as
x\rightarrow 0^{+}

\displaystyle W_{-1}(x)=\log|x|+\log|\log|x||+O\left(\frac{\log|\log|x||}{\log|x|}\right),

we get

\displaystyle W_{-1}\left(\frac{b\log(b)}{\sqrt{13}}\right)

\displaystyle=\log\left(b|\log(b)|\right)-\frac{1}{2}\log(13)+O(\log|\log(b|\log(b)|)|)

\displaystyle=\log(b)+\log|\log(b)|-\frac{1}{2}\log(13)+O(\log|\log(b|\log(b)|)|)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\log(13)+\log(b)\left(1+O\left(\frac{\log|\log(b)|}{|\log(b)|}\right)\right),

which gives us

\displaystyle e^{W_{-1}\left(\frac{b\log(b)}{\sqrt{13}}\right)}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{13}}b\,\exp\left(1+O\left(\frac{\log|\log(b)|}{|\log(b)|}\right)\right)\geq\lambda_{\ast}\,b,

where
0<\lambda_{\ast}<\infty
is a constant depending only on the Lambert function, by taking the supremum over all values
0<b<e^{-8}
. Let us estimate this constant explicitly. Let
-e^{-1}\leq x<0
. Then, we have by definition

\displaystyle W_{-1}(x)e^{W_{-1}(x)}=x

(4.57)

Notice that
W_{-1}(x)\leq-1<0
for all
-e^{-1}\leq x<0
. Takng the absolute value and the logarithm of (
4.57
), we get

\displaystyle\log|W_{-1}(x)|+W_{-1}(x)=\log|x|,

or

\displaystyle W_{-1}(x)=\log|x|-\log|W_{-1}(x)|\leq\log|x|

(4.58)

since
|W_{-1}(x)|\geq 1
. Now, thanks to (
4.57
), the Lambert function satisfies the following differential equation

\displaystyle W_{-1}^{\prime}(x)=\frac{W_{-1}(x)}{x(1+W_{-1}(x))}=\frac{1}{x}-\frac{1}{x(1+W_{-1}(x))}.

Now, let
\alpha>0
be a real number (to be determined later), and introduce the function

\displaystyle f(x)=W_{-1}(-x)-\log(x)+\alpha\log|\log(x)|

on
]0,e^{-1}[
. We have

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(x)=-\left(\frac{1}{(-x)}-\frac{1}{(-x)(1+W_{-1}(-x))}\right)-\frac{1}{x}+\frac{\alpha}{x\log(x)}=\frac{\alpha(1+W_{-1}(x))-\log(x)}{x\log(x)(1+W_{-1}(-x))}.

Taking
\alpha=2
yields

\displaystyle f^{\prime}(x)=\frac{|W_{-1}(-x)|-|\log(x)|+|W_{-1}(-x)|-2}{x|\log(x)|(1+W_{-1}(-x))}\geq 0

provided that
0<x<e^{-2}
. Indeed, (
4.58
) shows that
|W_{-1}(x)|=-W_{-1}(x)\geq|\log|x||
for all
-e^{-1}\leq x<0
, which shows that
W_{-1}(-x)-\log|x|\geq 0
for all
0<x<e^{-1}
. In particular,
|W_{-1}(-x)|-2\geq|\log(x)|-2\geq 0
for all
0<x<e^{-2}
. Therefore, we deduce that for all
0<x<e^{-2}
, we have

\displaystyle W_{-1}(-x)\geq\log(x)-2\log|\log(x)|,

and we finally have

\displaystyle W_{-1}(x)\geq\log|x|-2\log|\log|x||\quad\text{for all}\;\,-e^{-2}\leq x<0.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle W_{-1}\left(\frac{b\log(b)}{\sqrt{13}}\right)=\log(b)+\log|\log(b)|-\frac{1}{2}\log(13)-2\log\left|\log(b)+\log|\log(b)|-\frac{1}{2}\log(13)\right|,

and

\displaystyle e^{W_{-1}\left(\frac{b\log(b)}{\sqrt{13}}\right)}\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{13}}b\,e^{1+\frac{\log|\log(b)|}{\log(b)}-\frac{2\log\left|\log(b)+\log|\log(b)|-\frac{1}{2}\log(13)\right|}{\log(b)}}\geq\frac{1}{\sqrt{13}}b\,e^{1-\frac{\log|\log(b)|}{|\log(b)|}}\geq\frac{e^{1-\frac{3\log(2)}{4}}}{\sqrt{13}}b

for all
0<b<e^{-8}
.
In particular, our condition is verified provided that

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\frac{1}{2}\log\left(13\right)-\left(1-\frac{3\log(2)}{4}\right)=0.8023\cdots,

(4.59)

in which case

\displaystyle\left(2-\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)\log^{2}(b)+2\log(b)-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\left(2+\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)\log^{2}(a)-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\log(a)

\displaystyle+2\log(a)\log(b)\frac{\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\geq\frac{1}{2}\log^{2}(b)+2\log(b)\geq\frac{1}{4}\log^{2}(b)

provided that
0<b\leq e^{-8}
. Summarising, for all
0<b<e^{-8}
, provided that (
4.59
) holds, we deduce that

\displaystyle 2\pi\beta^{2}\log^{2}(b)\leq 8\pi\,b_{0}^{2}(b^{2}-a^{2})+\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx\leq 64\delta^{2}(\delta+1)\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx+\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx.

(4.60)

Finally, we have by (
4.50
) and (
4.60
)

\displaystyle\frac{16\pi\beta^{2}}{\gamma+1}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}\left(b\right)-\frac{\log(b)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{\log(a)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{16\pi\beta^{2}}{\gamma+1}b^{2}\left(\log^{2}\left(b\right)-\frac{\log(b)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)\leq\frac{16\pi\beta^{2}}{\gamma+1}b^{2}\left(\frac{3}{2}\log^{2}(b)+\frac{1}{(\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{12}{\gamma+1}\left(64\delta^{2}(2\delta+1)\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx+\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx\right)+\frac{16}{(\gamma+1)^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)\left(8\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{z}\psi|^{2}dx\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{768\,\delta^{2}(2\delta+1)}{\gamma+1}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx+\frac{12}{\gamma+1}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx+\frac{128}{(\gamma+1)^{3}}\left(\frac{1}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{z}^{2}\psi|^{2}dx

\displaystyle\leq\frac{768\,\delta(2\delta+1)}{\gamma+1}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx+\frac{32}{(\gamma+1)^{3}}\left(3(\gamma+1)^{2}+\frac{2}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx,

(4.61)

where we used the identity

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx=2\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{z}^{2}\psi|^{2}dx+2\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{z\overline{z}}^{2}\psi|^{2}dx=2\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{z}\psi|^{2}dx+\frac{1}{8}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx.

In order to obtain the final inequality, we proceed into three steps to emphasise the control we need with respect to the conformal class. First, thanks to (
4.1
) and, provided that (
4.39
) holds, we have

\displaystyle\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{((n+1)^{2}+4)}{n+1+\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{16\,\delta}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\delta}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx.

(4.62)

Then, (
4.1
) and (
4.46
), provided that (
4.45
) holds, we get

\displaystyle\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{|n|^{2}}{|n-1+\gamma|}|a_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right|\leq\frac{\pi}{\gamma}|a_{1}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{5\pi}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,1\right\}}\frac{n^{2}|n-1|}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2\delta(2\delta+1)^{2}}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\delta}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{5}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\left(\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx+\frac{1}{16}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\frac{5-5\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx\right)

(4.63)

Therefore, for all
\dfrac{1}{2}<\gamma,\delta<1
, thanks to the inequality (
4.1
), using (
4.49
), (
4.1
), (
4.1
), (
4.1
), and assuming that (
4.47
), (
4.52
) (
4.59
), (
4.39
), and (
4.45
) hold, namely

\displaystyle\log\left(\frac{b}{a}\right)\geq\max\left\{2,\frac{1}{2\delta-1}\log\left(4\delta\right),\frac{1}{4\delta}\log\left(\frac{2}{2-\sqrt{3}}\right),\frac{1}{4(1-\delta)}\log\left(1+\frac{8\delta(1-\delta)}{(2\delta-1)^{2}}\right),\frac{1}{4\delta}\log(8\delta(\delta+1))\right\}

(4.64)

and that
0<b\leq e^{-8}
, we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\psi|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}dx\leq\frac{32}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\int_{\Omega}|\partial_{z}^{2}\psi|^{2}dx+\frac{768\,\delta(2\delta+1)}{\gamma+1}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx

(4.65)

\displaystyle+\frac{32}{(\gamma+1)^{3}}\left(3(\gamma+1)^{2}+\frac{2}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{32\,\delta}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\delta}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{\delta}\right)^{4\delta}dx+\frac{1}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{4\delta(2\delta+1)^{2}}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\delta}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx

\displaystyle+\frac{10}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\left(\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx+\frac{1}{16}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\frac{5-5\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{768\,\delta(2\delta+1)}{\gamma+1}+\frac{4\delta(8+(2\delta+1)^{2})}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\delta}}\right)\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx

\displaystyle+\left(32\left(\frac{1}{(\gamma+1)^{3}}\left(3(\gamma+1)^{2}+\frac{2}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\right)+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{1}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\left(18+5\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\frac{5-5\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}}\right)\right)\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx.

(4.66)

Notice that the hypothesis
0<a<b\leq e^{-8}
is not restrictive since all inequalities enjoy the same scaling, and yields half of our inequality; namely,

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\psi|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}dx\leq C_{\gamma,\delta}\left(\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{|x|}{b}\right)^{4\delta}dx+\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx\right).

(4.67)

Step 3:
Estimation of

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\psi|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\gamma}dx.

Recall that

\displaystyle\mathrm{rad}\left(|\nabla\psi|^{2}\right)=\frac{1}{|z|^{2}}\bigg\{\left(\alpha+2\beta|z|^{2}\log|z|+(\beta+2b_{0})|z|^{2}\right)^{2}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|n|^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2n}

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}((n+1)^{2}+4)|b_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2(n+2)}+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}n(n+1)\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}|z|^{2(n+1)}\right)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|z|^{2}}\bigg\{4\left(\alpha^{2}+4\beta^{2}|z|^{4}\log^{2}|z|+(\beta+2b_{0})^{2}|z|^{4}\right)+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|n|^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2n}

\displaystyle+2\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}((n+1)^{2}+2)|b_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2(n+2)}\bigg\}.

Therefore, we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{|\nabla\psi|^{2}}{|x|^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{2\gamma}dx\leq 4\pi\int_{a}^{b}\frac{1}{r^{3}}\bigg\{2\alpha^{2}+8\beta^{2}r^{4}\log^{2}(r)+2(\beta+2b_{0})^{2}r^{4}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}|n|^{2}|a_{n}|^{2}r^{2n}

\displaystyle+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}((n+1)^{2}+2)|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2(n+2)}\bigg\}\left(\frac{a}{r}\right)^{2\gamma}dr=\frac{4\pi\alpha^{2}}{\gamma+1}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{16\pi\beta^{2}}{1-\gamma}\left(b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\log(b)+\frac{1}{2(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right.

\displaystyle\left.-a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{1}{1-\gamma}\log(a)+\frac{1}{2(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\right)+\frac{4\pi(\beta+2b_{0})^{2}}{1-\gamma}\left(b^{2}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-a^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{|n|^{2}}{n-1-\gamma}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-a^{2(n-1)}\right)

\displaystyle+2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}^{\ast}}\frac{(n+1)^{2}+2}{n+1+\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-a^{2(n+1)}\right).

(4.68)

We start by estimating thanks to (
4.1
)

\displaystyle\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,-1\right\}}\frac{(n+1)^{2}+2}{n+1+\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-a^{2(n+1)}\right)\leq\frac{3}{\gamma}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,-1\right\}}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n+1)}-a^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right|

\displaystyle=\frac{3}{\gamma}\left(\sum_{n\geq 0}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+1)}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1)}\right)+\sum_{n\geq 2}|n-1||b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1+\gamma}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{\gamma}\left(\sum_{n\geq 0}|n+1||b_{n}|^{2}b^{2(n+1)}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1)}\right)+\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\sum_{n\geq 2}|n-1||b_{-n}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{2(n-1)}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(n+1)}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{3}{8\pi\,\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx.

(4.69)

Now, recalling (
4.1
)

\displaystyle 2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,1\right\}}\frac{n^{2}|n-1|}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}|b^{2(n-1)}-a^{2(n-1)}|\leq 2\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx+\frac{1}{8}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\frac{5-5\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx,

we get

\displaystyle\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,1\right\}}\frac{|n|^{2}}{n-1-\gamma}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-a^{2(n-1)}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5}{1-\gamma}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}\setminus\left\{0,1\right\}}\frac{n^{2}|n-1|}{n^{2}+1}|a_{n}|^{2}\left|b^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}-a^{2(n-1)}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{5}{\pi(1-\gamma)}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}}\left(2\int_{\Omega}|\nabla^{2}\psi|^{2}dx+\frac{1}{8}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\frac{5-5\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}+2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}}{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)^{3}}\int_{\Omega}(\Delta\psi)^{2}dx\right).

(4.70)

Then, recalling that

\displaystyle\mathrm{rad}(\psi^{2})

\displaystyle=\alpha^{2}\log^{2}|z|+2\alpha\,a_{0}\log|z|+2\alpha\beta|z|^{2}\log^{2}|z|+2(\alpha\,b_{0}+\beta\,a_{0})|z|^{2}\log|z|+\beta^{2}|z|^{4}\log^{2}|z|

\displaystyle+2\beta\,b_{0}|z|^{4}\log|z|+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}|a_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2n}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}|b_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2(n+2)}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)|z|^{2(n+1)},

we get

\displaystyle\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\gamma}dx=2\pi\int_{a}^{b}\frac{1}{r^{3}}\bigg\{\alpha^{2}\log^{2}(r)+2\alpha\,a_{0}\log(r)+2\alpha\beta r^{2}\log^{2}(r)+2\left(\alpha\,b_{0}+\beta\,a_{0}\right)r^{2}\log(r)

\displaystyle+\beta^{2}r^{4}\log^{2}(r)+2\beta\,b_{0}r^{4}\log(r)+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}|a_{n}|^{2}|z|^{2n}+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}|b_{n}|^{2}r^{2(n+2)}+\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)r^{2(n+1)}\bigg\}\left(\frac{a}{r}\right)^{4\gamma}dr

\displaystyle=\frac{\pi\alpha^{2}}{2\gamma+1}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{\log(a)}{2\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\frac{\log(b)}{2\gamma+1}+\frac{1}{2(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi\alpha\,a_{0}}{2\gamma+1}\left(\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\log(a)-\frac{1}{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)-\frac{1}{b^{2}}\left(\log(b)-\frac{1}{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi\alpha\beta}{\gamma}\left(\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{\log(a)}{2\gamma^{2}}+\frac{1}{4\gamma^{2}}\right)-\left(\log^{2}(b)-\frac{\log(b)}{2\gamma^{2}}+\frac{1}{4\gamma^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi\left(\alpha\,b_{0}+\beta\,a_{0}\right)}{\gamma}\left(\log(a)-\frac{1}{4\gamma}-\left(\log(b)-\frac{1}{4\gamma}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{\pi\beta^{2}}{2\gamma-1}\left(a^{2}\left(\log^{2}(a)-\frac{\log(a)}{2\gamma-1}+\frac{1}{2(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\right)-b^{2}\left(\log^{2}(b)-\frac{\log(b)}{2\gamma-1}+\frac{1}{2(2\gamma-1)^{2}}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2\pi\beta\,b_{0}}{2\gamma-1}\left(a^{2}\left(\log(a)-\frac{1}{2\gamma-1}\right)-b^{2}\left(\log(b)-\frac{1}{2\gamma-1}\right)\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{1}{n-1-2\gamma}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-a^{2(n-1)}\right)+\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{1}{n+1-2\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-a^{2(n+1)}\right)

\displaystyle+2\pi\sum_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\frac{1}{n-2\gamma}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\left(b^{2n}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-a^{2n}\right).

As previously, we need to bound
b_{-1}
and
a_{1}
, since those frequencies are not detected by the second derivative. For all
n\in\mathbb{Z}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n-1-2\gamma}|a_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n-1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-a^{2(n-1)}\right)+\frac{1}{n+1-2\gamma}|b_{n}|^{2}\left(b^{2(n+1)}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-a^{2(n+1)}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{n-2\gamma}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{n}\overline{b_{n}}\right)\left(b^{2n}\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-a^{2n}\right)\leq\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\gamma}dx.

(4.71)

Applying (
4.1
) to
n=-1
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)}|a_{-1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(\gamma+1)}\right)+\frac{1}{2\gamma}|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{2\gamma+1}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}\overline{b_{-1}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)\leq\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\gamma}dx.

Now, we estimate

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)}|a_{-1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(\gamma+1)}\right)+\frac{1}{2\gamma}|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{2\gamma+1}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}\overline{b_{-1}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)

\displaystyle\geq|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(\frac{1}{2\gamma}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)-\frac{2(\gamma+1)}{(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\frac{\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)^{2}}{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(\gamma+1)}}\right)

This is exactly the quantity appearing in (
4.41
), and we deduce by (
4.46
) that for all
\dfrac{1}{2}<\delta<1
, provided that (
4.43
) holds, we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2(\gamma+1)}|a_{-1}|^{2}\frac{1}{a^{4}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4(\gamma+1)}\right)+\frac{1}{2\gamma}|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{2\gamma+1}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{-1}\overline{b_{-1}}\right)\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2(2\gamma+1)}\right)\geq\frac{|b_{-1}|^{2}}{2\gamma(2\gamma+1)^{2}}\left(1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right),

which implies that

\displaystyle\pi|b_{-1}|^{2}\leq\frac{2\gamma(2\gamma+1)^{2}}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\gamma}dx

and more generally, for all
\dfrac{1}{2}<\delta<1
, if (
4.45
) holds, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\pi}{\gamma}|b_{-1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{2\delta(2\delta+1)^{2}}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\delta}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\delta}dx.

(4.72)

Likewise, taking
n=1
yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\gamma}|a_{1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}\right)+\frac{1}{2(1-\gamma)}|b_{1}|^{2}b^{4}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4}\right)-\frac{2}{2\gamma-1}\mathrm{Re}\,\left(a_{1}\overline{b_{1}}\right)b^{2}\left(\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\gamma}-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\pi}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\gamma}dx.

Once more, we recognise (up to changing
b_{-1}
by
a_{1}
and
a_{-1}
by
b_{1}
and the weight in the integral) (
4.1
). Therefore, for all
\dfrac{1}{2}<\delta<1
, if (
4.39
) holds, we get by (
4.40
)

\displaystyle\frac{4\pi}{\gamma}|a_{1}|^{2}\left(1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}\right)\leq\frac{16\,\delta}{\gamma}\frac{1-\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{2\gamma}}{1-2\left(\frac{a}{b}\right)^{4\delta}}\int_{\Omega}\frac{\psi^{2}}{|x|^{4}}\left(\frac{a}{|x|}\right)^{4\delta}dx.

(4.73)
